Abstract: AraGenre is a shared task on hierarchical, definition-guided Arabic genre classification, motivated by the limited availability of annotated data in Arabic and other low-resource languages. Systems assign each Arabic text segment both a broad communicative genre and a fine-grained specific genre. The released training and development sets contain limited, primarily synthetic and controlled examples, whereas the hidden final benchmark contains noisier naturally occurring text spanning Modern Standard Arabic, Classical Arabic, and multiple dialects. Participants received natural-language definitions for 74 previously unseen specific genres, creating a zero-shot label generalisation setting in which systems had to infer class semantics rather than memorise fixed label-feature associations. The task attracted 46 registrations and 373 submissions, with 17 teams completing the final evaluation. Thakaa ranked first with a Hierarchical Macro F1 of 0.7352, followed by HoangPhong (HP) with 0.7169 and NAMAA with 0.7013. The results show strong broad-genre recognition but a substantial gap in fine-grained classification under linguistic and domain variation.

</details>


### [38] [PRISM-VLM: A Multi-Axis Discriminative Benchmark for Compact Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27395)
*Sanghee Park,Kee-Eung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PRISM-VLM多轴判别性基准，在七个轴向上评分紧凑型视觉语言模型并汇总为PScore，比单轴基准更能可靠区分模型并揭示行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准继承前沿导向设计，将模型压缩为单一准确率数值，在饱和套件上区分度不足，在较难套件上把模型挤入低分段，无法反映紧凑型VLM的真实差异。

Method: 构建多轴判别性基准PRISM-VLM，对每个条目沿七个轴（覆盖任务质量、行为鲁棒性、能力瓶颈等常见失败模式）评分，汇总为单一PScore；条目复用自15个公开基准。

Result: 在过去两年的紧凑型VLM上，PScore在条目级配对bootstrap下比既往单轴基准更可靠地区分模型对，并揭示被平均值掩盖的行为差异；即使PScore统计上不可区分的模型，在各轴画像上也差异显著，尤其谄媚性几乎与单提示准确率正交。

Conclusion: 多轴判别性评测能更好地刻画紧凑型VLM的行为差异，未来将发布完整流程、提示与逐条目标注。

Abstract: Compact vision-language models (VLMs) now power a growing share of multimodal applications. The benchmarks used to compare them, however, inherit a frontier-centric design: each model is reduced to a single accuracy number, narrowing the inter-model gap on saturated suites and pressing models into low-score bands on harder ones. We introduce PRISM-VLM, a multi-axis discriminative benchmark that scores every item along seven axes covering the recurring failure modes (task quality, behavioral robustness, and capability bottlenecks) and combines them into a single PScore, with items recycled from fifteen public benchmarks. Across compact VLMs from the past two years, PScore separates model pairs more reliably than prior single-axis benchmarks under an item-level paired bootstrap, and surfaces behavioral differences these benchmarks average away. Even models with statistically indistinguishable PScores diverge sharply along the per-axis profile, particularly on sycophancy, which is nearly orthogonal to single-prompt accuracy. We will release the full pipeline, prompts, and per-item annotations.

</details>


### [39] [When Parallel Drafter Meets Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27396)
*Fuliang Liu,Xue Li,Kun Qian,Zhibin Wang,Wanchun Dou,Wenyuan Yu,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DPara提出一种并行推测解码框架，通过预计算每个接受边界的草稿表示，保证验证与骨干前向完全重叠，消除错误猜测回退，实现3.21x-3.52x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSpark等并行草稿器虽提升了推测解码效率，但草稿阶段仍在每轮关键路径上串行执行。现有并行推测解码（PSD）方法需预先猜测接受前缀和bonus token，一旦猜错整个批次会退回串行草稿，导致概率性回退问题。

Method: DPara复用高效并行草稿器，同时保证每轮骨干与验证重叠。具体地，在目标模型验证时，其扩散骨干为每个接受边界预计算草稿表示（bonus留空）；轻量自回归头随后结合已揭示的验证结果与匹配的预计算表示，几乎即时生成下一轮草稿token，从而将主导的骨干前向与验证完全并行，仅保留可忽略的头开销为串行。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B上，覆盖七个数学、编程和对话基准测试，DPara相比自回归解码平均加速3.21x和3.52x，超越最强的串行和并行推测解码基线。

Conclusion: DPara通过预计算接受边界表示并让轻量头与验证结果结合，彻底消除PSD的概率性回退，实现了稳定高效的骨干-验证重叠，在多个基准上显著超越现有推测解码方法。

Abstract: DSpark-style parallel drafters have made speculative decoding highly effective, yet their draft phase remains serialized on the critical path of every round. Parallel speculative decoding (PSD) overlaps drafting with verification, yet existing methods must guess the accepted prefix and bonus token in advance: a wrong guess reverts the whole batch to serial drafting. We present DPara, a PSD framework that reuses effective parallel drafters yet guarantees backbone--verification overlap in every round, thereby eliminating this probabilistic fallback altogether. While the target verifies, DPara's diffusion backbone precomputes draft representations for every acceptance boundary with the bonus left unspecified; a lightweight autoregressive head then combines the revealed verification outcome with the matching precomputed representation to emit the next round's draft tokens almost instantly---fully parallelizing the dominant backbone forward with verification and leaving only the negligible head cost serial. Experiments on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B across seven math, coding, and chat benchmarks show that DPara achieves average speedups of $3.21\times$ and $3.52\times$ over autoregressive decoding, surpassing the strongest serial and parallel speculative decoding baselines alike.

</details>


### [40] [EviStreams: Human-in-the-Loop AI Data Extraction for Systematic Reviews in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27418)
*Sai Karthik Kosuri,Ankita Shashikant Bhosale,Michael Glick,Alonso Carrasco-Labra,Chris Callison-Burch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviStreams 是一个开源无代码平台，让综述团队在协议化流程中控制 AI 辅助的数据提取，强调字段规范比模型选择更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述支撑临床指南，但数据提取是专家劳动瓶颈：需双人独立提取、裁决分歧并保留可审计记录。LLM 可辅助，但必须契合既有综述协议并保证可复现。

Method: EviStreams 为在线开源无代码平台，覆盖三阶段：程序设计（运行前结构化分解审批）、字段规范（从试点校准类型化字段）、提取预测（审阅者盲法双审与裁决）。专家通过表单构建器定义类型化字段而非提示词，对上传 PDF 运行提取，逐值查看支持段落，并将盲法双审解析为可审计的共识导出。

Result: 在四个临床语料库和三个前沿模型家族上评估（随系统发布），显示提取质量受字段规范影响远大于模型选择。

Conclusion: EviStreams 已在 https://evistreams.com/demo 上线，并以 Apache-2.0 许可发布。

Abstract: Systematic reviews underpin clinical guidelines, yet their data-extraction step is a major expert-labor bottleneck bound by a protocolized workflow: two reviewers extract each study independently, an adjudicator resolves disagreements, and the team keeps an auditable record of how every value was produced. Large language models can assist with extraction, but that assistance must fit established review protocols and preserve reproducibility. We present EviStreams, a live, open-source, no-code web platform that puts review teams in control of AI-assisted extraction at three key stages: program design (a structured decomposition approved before any code runs), field specification (typed field definitions calibrated from a pilot), and extracted predictions (reviewer-blinded dual review with adjudication). Working through a form builder, a domain expert defines typed fields rather than prompts, runs extraction over uploaded PDFs, inspects every value alongside the supporting passage it came from, and resolves a reviewer-blinded dual review into an auditable consensus export. An evaluation across four clinical corpora and three frontier model families, released with the system, shows that extraction quality is shaped far more by the field specification than by the choice of model. EviStreams is live at https://evistreams.com/demo and released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [41] [ThaiTrees: Thai Syntactic Dependency Trees Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27558)
*Attapol T. Rutherford,Papatchol Thientong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ThaiTrees，一个3.42亿token的泰语依存句法树库，覆盖新闻、维基百科、口语和社会媒体，并提供可复现的清洗、处理与解析流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自然语言句法模式需要大规模已解析语料，但人工标注成本高、难扩展；泰语虽有人工标注的依存树库，却缺乏大规模自动解析语料库用于定量句法研究。

Method: 构建ThaiTrees语料库，从新闻、维基百科、口语转录和社会媒体抽取3.42亿token；开发可复现的pipeline进行泰语文本清洗、处理，并在Universal Dependencies框架下进行依存解析。

Result: 语料库使语法关系可搜索，支持句法分布研究；发布了频率词典和机器可读的CoNLL-U解析文件，适用于AI辅助和传统程序化分析。

Conclusion: ThaiTrees填补了泰语大规模自动解析语料的空白，为定量句法研究和语法关系检索提供了可复现、可扩展的资源。

Abstract: Studying syntactic patterns in naturally occurring language requires a large parsed corpus, but manual annotation is costly and difficult to scale. Thai has a manually annotated dependency treebank for training and evaluating parsers, but lacks a large automatically parsed corpus for quantitative syntactic research. We present ThaiTrees, a 342M-token corpus drawn from news, Wikipedia, spoken transcripts, and social media. We develop a reproducible pipeline for cleaning, processing, and parsing Thai text under the Universal Dependencies framework. The resulting corpus makes grammatical relations searchable and supports the study of syntactic distributions. We release a frequency lexicon and CoNLL-U parses in machine-readable formats suitable for both AI-assisted and conventional programmatic analysis.

</details>


### [42] [MWE-ECL: Recoverable Long-Range Context Does Not Always Override Local Lexical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27590)
*Wei He,Aline Villavicencio,Rodrigo Wilkens,Zhenyun Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MWE-ECL 诊断，检验远距离语篇锚点虽可被检索到、却不一定改变多词表达的局部解读，即"可恢复性≠行为影响"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文评估常测试能否恢复远距离证据，但可恢复并不等于该证据会影响模型决策。作者想验证：熟悉的固定表达存在局部偏好解读，远距离锚点可能显式可检索，却无法改变该局部解读，且这种失败应集中于锚点与无锚点默认冲突时。

Method: 引入 MWE-ECL 双语诊断，用匹配的锚点检索提示、无锚点先验提示和解读提示，分别测量显式可恢复性、模型观察到的默认偏好和锚点条件下的决策。在共享 0-128K 网格上测试八个英文部署面板，并对 DeepSeek V4 Pro 做同调用控制，对 DeepSeek V4 Flash 做 512K/1M 的长上下文与干扰线索测试，另报告 10 族中文子集。

Result: 英文面板中，冲突项检索控制准确率 0.989-1.000，冲突覆盖率为 0.806-1.000（条件于正确检索后 0.809-1.000），先前正确决策保持率 0.977-1.000。DeepSeek V4 Pro 同调用控制仍显差距（检索 1.000 vs 解读 0.900-0.920）；另两个模型差距较小或不存在，限制了普适性。Flash 在 512K 和 1M 仍保持完美检索但解读更低；干扰线索对无锚点先验的影响远大于检索，跨模型线索效应异质。中文子集呈类似描述性差距，但部分模型检索不完美，无法归因于"仅整合失败"。

Conclusion: MWE-ECL 评估的是显式可恢复的远距离上下文能否改变相互竞争的局部语义决策，表明可恢复性与行为影响之间存在系统性缺口。

Abstract: Long-context evaluations often test whether a model can recover distant evidence, but recoverability does not guarantee behavioral influence. We test the prediction that a distant discourse anchor can remain explicitly recoverable yet fail to change the locally preferred reading of a familiar multiword expression; such failures should concentrate when the model's no-anchor default conflicts with the anchor, while prior-correct decisions remain largely preserved. We introduce Multiword Expression Effective Context Length (MWE-ECL), a bilingual diagnostic whose matched anchor-retrieval, no-anchor prior, and interpretation prompts measure explicit recoverability, model-observed defaults, and anchor-conditioned decisions, respectively. Across eight English deployment panels on a shared 0-128K grid, retrieval-control accuracy on prior-conflict items is 0.989-1.000, prior-conflict override spans 0.806-1.000 (0.809-1.000 after conditioning on correct retrieval), and preservation of prior-correct decisions remains 0.977-1.000. A same-call control querying retrieval and interpretation in one prompt reproduces the gap for DeepSeek V4 Pro (1.000 retrieval versus 0.900-0.920 interpretation), showing that separate invocations are not its sole explanation; smaller or absent gaps in the other two models bound its generality. For DeepSeek V4 Flash, separate prompt-fit tests retain perfect retrieval with lower interpretation at 512K and 1M, while foil-consistent cues shift the no-anchor prior far more than retrieval; cross-model cue effects are heterogeneous. A separately reported 10-family Chinese subset shows similar descriptive gaps, but imperfect retrieval for some models prevents an integration-only attribution. MWE-ECL therefore evaluates whether explicitly recoverable distant context changes a competing local semantic decision.

</details>


### [43] [When Context Misleads: In-context Learning with Jurisdiction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27603)
*Pei-lin Li,Qingle Liu,Junyang Feng,Siyu Li,Sunqi Fan,Xin-Sheng Chen,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出FakeContextBench基准，揭示现有ICL后训练忽视"上下文权威"问题，并提出J-ICL框架，使ICL能力与抗欺骗上下文能力可共同提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL后训练方法擅长从演示中提取模式，却常忽视"上下文权威"——即判断上下文信息是否应主导最终答案的能力。仅靠大规模预训练不足以可靠区分上下文权威，且主流ICL微调方法反而增加对误导性上下文的易感性，使真值准确率最多下降14.95个百分点。

Method: 构建FakeContextBench基准，涵盖七个领域的伪科学主张；提出Jurisdiction In-Context Learning (J-ICL)后训练框架，将上下文验证纳入训练目标。

Result: 在四个模型骨干上，J-ICL使ICLEval平均提升5.84个百分点，真值准确率较基模型提升9.20个百分点；相比MetaICL和Symbol Tuning，Reality Rate平均提升18.09个百分点。

Conclusion: ICL能力与抗欺骗上下文能力可以同时提升，J-ICL有效解决了二者之间的权衡问题。基准已开源。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) has become a cornerstone of modern LLM deployment. However, existing ICL post-training methods have a critical blind spot: they excel at extracting patterns from demonstrations while often neglecting context authority, the ability to determine whether contextual information should govern the final answer. To benchmark this capability, we introduce FakeContextBench, which contains pseudoscientific claims across seven domains. Our evaluation of commercial and open-source models shows that large-scale pre-training alone is insufficient for reliable context-authority discrimination. Moreover, prevalent ICL fine-tuning methods can increase susceptibility to misleading context, reducing reality accuracy by up to 14.95 percentage points relative to the base model. To address this trade-off, we propose Jurisdiction In-Context Learning (J-ICL), a post-training framework that incorporates context validation into the training objective. Across four model backbones, J-ICL improves ICLEval by an average of 5.84 percentage points and reality accuracy by 9.20 points over the corresponding base models. It also raises the Reality Rate by an average of 18.09 points relative to MetaICL and Symbol Tuning. These results demonstrate that ICL capability and resistance to deceptive context can be improved together. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/peilin717/FakeContext-Bench.

</details>


### [44] [The Path Matters: Evaluating Small Language Models Beyond Answer Accuracy in KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27669)
*Eduin E. Hernandez,Sergio A. Diaz,Luis F. Garcia,Nurassyl Askar,Stefano Rini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过THESEUS框架隔离并评估SLM在知识图谱导航中的推理能力，发现准确率与路径保真度有时相互矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端KG问答耦合了图访问、搜索、导航、推理与答案生成，难以判断SLM能否忠实执行问题隐含的推理路径，也难以将失败归因于导航而非其他环节。

Method: 使用THESEUS导航与可追溯框架，以冻结的SLM作为局部动作策略；每跳环境暴露合法出边动作，模型选择可执行图动作并决定是否停止，不进行任务特定参数更新、模型控制束搜索或自由文本答案生成。

Result: 在Kinship与MQuAKE-ST数据集上，规模相近的本地模型在答案准确率（Hits@1）与路径保真度（PED）上差异显著，且两指标有时偏好不同模型；单个示范轨迹的影响也随模型而变，可能提升或损害导航。

Conclusion: 仅用端点准确率评估SLM图推理不足，需同时关注路径保真度等轨迹指标。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly paired with knowledge graphs (KGs), yet end-to-end KG question answering conflates graph access, search, navigation, reasoning, and answer generation. This coupling makes it difficult both to determine whether an SLM can faithfully execute the reasoning path implied by a question and to attribute failures to navigation rather than to other stages of the pipeline. We isolate this capability by employing the THESEUS navigation and traceability framework and using frozen, off-the-shelf SLMs as local action policies. At each hop, the environment exposes the legal outgoing graph actions, and the model selects one executable graph action and decides whether to stop, without task-specific parameter updates, model-controlled beam search, or free-form answer generation. This controlled setting allows us to evaluate terminal-answer accuracy with Hits@1 together with path fidelity, using Path Edit Distance (PED) as the primary trajectory metric. Across the Kinship and MQuAKE-ST KGQAs, similarly sized local models differ substantially in answer accuracy and path fidelity, with the two metrics sometimes favoring different models. This model-dependent behavior also extends to prompting, as a single demonstrated trajectory can improve or degrade navigation depending on the model. These results motivate evaluating SLM graph reasoning beyond endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [45] [Same Scores, Different Decisions: Evaluating JEV and Language Models for Legal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27678)
*Fan Zhang,Yankai Chen,Zhuohan Xie,Yixi Zhou,Sijia Peng,Lei Fan,Xinhua Ji,Cunyuan Zheng,Huangyong Shan,Philip S. Yu,Xue Liu,Yu Chen,Preslav Nakov,Songwei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在ContractNLI上比较Jev与九个大语言模型，综合评估成本、响应时间、平均正确率及跨条件/重复请求的正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合准确率会掩盖个体决策变化，且重复一致性不能保证正确性，因此需要更细粒度的评估。

Method: 控制变量比较假设可见性、请求输出及输出顺序，同时固定合同与目标判断。

Result: Jev成本最低、响应时间中位数最短，托管模型基线准确率更高；基线排名与跨条件正确率排名不一致，且发现补偿性纠正与退化及持续错误。

Conclusion: 应同时评估成本与响应时间，以及请求配置变化时个体判断是否保持正确。

Abstract: Contract inference requires multiple judgments about a shared document, but aggregate accuracy can conceal changes in the individual decisions. Repeated agreement is also insufficient: a model may consistently return the wrong answer. In this paper, we compare Jev with nine language models on ContractNLI, evaluating inference cost, response time, average correctness, and correctness across repeated request conditions. Controlled comparisons vary hypothesis visibility, requested outputs, and output order while keeping the contract and target judgment fixed. Jev has the lowest cost and median response time among the evaluated configurations, while hosted language models achieve higher baseline accuracy. Rankings by baseline accuracy differ from rankings by correctness across every condition and repeat, although small differences in the latter do not establish a general stability advantage. Development diagnostics further reveal compensating corrections and regressions, as well as persistent errors. These findings motivate evaluating cost and response time alongside whether individual judgments remain correct as the request configuration changes. Code: https://github.com/ZF-Utokyo/Jev-Benchmark

</details>


### [46] [Consequential Behaviour and Representational Fairness in the Validation of Synthetic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27690)
*Florian Kutzner,Celina Kacperski,Laura de Molière,Edoardo Chidichimo,Min Jun Jung,Felix Patrick Sedgwick Wallis,James Kunling He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出用LLM合成调查受访者的验证方式存在问题，提出包含两层要求与三项正义维度的验证框架，并配以报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界与学界用大语言模型驱动的合成受访者替代真人样本，但其合成人口需要针对真实数据做验证，现有做法多是与人调查的临时性比较。受行为科学中"意图—行为差距"启发，作者认为这些验证在多数应用场景下测错了对象——决策者委托合成研究是为了预测真实的后果性行为。

Method: 提出一个验证框架，含两项要求：第一，每个有效性主张必须说明其与人数据的对应层级（样本是否预测所代表人群的行为、覆盖四类诊断中的哪一类——位置、离散度、反应过程与结构、是否对照实验效应）；第二，研究者必须报告子组的有效性主张，因为子组常最受后果性决策影响，而聚合精度会掩盖其被误代表的问题。框架还将分配、程序与承认三种正义维度操作化为可测量量，并把"个体内反事实实验"设为验证要求。

Result: 将该框架应用于电动汽车充电电价场景进行演示，并给出可供研究者使用的报告清单。

Conclusion: 验证应聚焦于后果性行为的预测而非仅意图一致性，需明确对应层级、覆盖子组、纳入正义维度与个体内反事实实验，并通过报告清单提升有效性主张的可信度。

Abstract: Researchers in industry and academia use synthetic survey respondents powered by large language models as substitutes for human samples. These synthetic populations require validation against real-world data, so researchers often address them using ad hoc comparisons with human surveys. Inspired by the intention-behaviour gap in behavioural science, we argue that these validations test the wrong thing for most applied cases where decision makers commission synthetic research to anticipate consequential behaviour. To address this problem, we propose a validation framework with two requirements. First, every validity claim must state its level of correspondence with human data: does the sample predict what the represented people do, which of four diagnostics (location, dispersion, response process and structure) does the validation address, and does the validation compare against experimental effects? Second, researchers must report validity claims for subgroups, since these groups are often the most affected by consequential decisions and aggregate accuracy hides their misrepresentation. Our validation framework operationalises three justice dimensions (distributional, procedural, and recognition) as measurable quantities and defines within-persona counterfactual experiments as a validation requirement. We then apply the framework to electric vehicle charging tariffs, before closing with a reporting checklist that researchers can use to make convincing validity claims.

</details>


### [47] [Improving LLM-based Autonomous Web Agents with Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27770)
*Zhitong Guo,Jing Yu Koh,Ruiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM网页智能体的HTML输入冗余问题，提出基于检索的策略过滤无关上下文，训练DeBERTa/T5模型对HTML元素排序，提升WebArena任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始HTML源码冗长且含大量无关细节，受限于LLM有限的上下文窗口，直接作为输入会阻碍智能体的多步推理与决策能力。

Method: 先在WebArena上复现GPT-3.5与LLaMA-2-70B基线并分析失败模式；提出两种检索策略；基于DeBERTa和T5构建HTML元素相关性排序模型，用Mind2Web轨迹数据微调并迁移到WebArena；另开发零样本ColBERT检索器。

Result: DeBERTa检索模型将LLaMA-2-70B智能体在WebArena上的成功率从1.97%提升至2.96%；零样本ColBERT检索器在Mind2Web召回率为0.52，在WebArena为0.47。

Conclusion: 通过检索过滤无关HTML上下文可有效提升LLM网页智能体的任务表现，且模型可从Mind2Web迁移至WebArena。

Abstract: Autonomous web agents, powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), have garnered significant attention for automating various web-based tasks with multi-step reasoning and decision-making capabilities. An open research question in the development of these agents lies in the format of the webpage input. Raw HTML source code, with its extensive and often irrelevant details, poses difficulties for LLMs with limited context windows. To address this challenge, we first reproduce baseline models such as GPT-3.5 and LLaMA-2-70B on the WebArena (Zhou et al., 2023) benchmark, identifying common failure modes. We then propose two retrieval strategies to filter out irrelevant context for LLM agents. We develop DeBERTa-based and T5-based models that rank HTML elements by their relevance to the task. We fine-tune them on Mind2Web trajectory data and transfer them to WebArena. Experiments show that our DeBERTa-based model improves the success rate of the LLaMA-2-70B LLM agent on WebArena from 1.97% to 2.96%. Moreover, we develop a zero-shot ColBERT-based retriever that is able to retrieve the ground-truth element with a recall of 0.52 on Mind2Web and 0.47 on WebArena.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Unsafe Detection: Counterfactually Anchored Evidence Attribution for Multi-Turn LLM Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27773)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Kazi Aminul Islam,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对多轮对话中对抗意图分散导致的护栏失效问题，构建了含行为验证与分层证据监督的多轮数据集，并提出轻量级层次归因模型，能检测安全违规并将责任归因到具体用户轮次与token片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏评估框架只关注最终结果（判定用户请求安全与否），无法应对对抗意图分散在多轮对话中的多轮失效场景，因此需要超越单纯检测，识别推动对话走向不安全轨迹的具体轮次和token。

Method: 构建带行为验证和分层证据监督的多轮数据集，包含1762个对话（对抗对话、良性孪生、含高风险词汇的良性变体）；训练轻量级层次归因模型，预测安全违规并归因到贡献性用户轮次与token span。

Result: 检测性能强（F1=0.988）；移除归因得分最高的15% token可使对抗分类置信度下降51.1%；在含高风险词汇的良性对话上假阳性低于1%（边界良性及良性高风险词汇均低于1%），而基于关键词的表面风险基线分别为37.3%和94.7%；独立人工标注支持归因效果，84.5%对抗案例中人工认定的证据轮次出现在模型归因前五的轮次内。

Conclusion: 该工作证明护栏评估不应止于安全/不安全判定，而应定位导致不安全轨迹的轮次与token；所提数据集与层次归因模型在保持低假阳性的同时实现了高检测精度和有效归因。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) move from conversational assistants to advanced agentic systems, guardrail failures can convert adversarial intents into harmful executions. However, most guardrail evaluation frameworks focus only on the result and assess whether a user request is safe or unsafe. This approach is insufficient for multi-turn failures, where adversarial intent is distributed across multiple turns. This motivates us to go beyond detection to identify the turns and tokens that push the conversation toward unsafe trajectories. To support this, we construct a multi-turn dataset with behavioral validation and tiered evidence supervision. The dataset contains 1,762 conversations, including adversarial conversations, benign twins, and benign variants with high-risk vocabulary. We train a lightweight hierarchical attribution model that predicts safety violations and attributes them to contributing user turns and token spans. The model achieves strong detection performance (F1=0.988), and removing the top 15% of attributed tokens reduces the adversarial classification confidence by 51.1%. The model preserves low false positive rates on benign conversations with high-risk vocabulary, with false positives below 1% on both borderline benign and benign high-risk vocabulary conversations, compared to 37.3% and 94.7% for a keyword-based surface-risk baseline. Independent human annotation supports the model's attribution performance, with the top-five attributed turns containing a human-identified evidence-bearing turn in 84.5% of adversarial cases.

</details>


### [49] ["AI Is Turning Too Human": How Teenagers Experience and Negotiate AI in Everyday Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27824)
*Jianfeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析2023-2026年r/teenagers上AI相关讨论，揭示青少年对AI在日常生活中的角色与边界的多维协商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正快速进入青少年的生活，但其应用速度超越了关于青少年自身如何体验、理解与协商AI角色的证据。研究旨在填补这一空白，从青少年视角出发理解其对AI的认知与态度。

Method: 分析2023年1月至2026年7月r/teenagers论坛的AI相关讨论。采用经验证的关键词检索与人在回路（human-in-the-loop）、LLM辅助的主题分析，共编码11,083条帖子。

Result: AI相关讨论随时间显著增加，识别出八个相互关联的体验领域。日常与社交使用最为普遍（36.8%），讨论逐渐转向真实性、个人控制、安全及未来人类角色等议题。

Conclusion: 青少年的AI使用不仅是技术采纳，更是对AI在生活中的位置与边界的协商。需提供适龄的AI素养教育、心理与社会支持，以及保护其能动性、隐私、关系与发展机会的AI系统与政策。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly entering adolescents' everyday lives during a critical period of cognitive, social and emotional development. Yet its adoption is outpacing evidence on how adolescents themselves experience, understand and negotiate its expanding role in their lives. We examined AI-related discourse on r/teenagers from January 2023 to July 2026 using validated keyword-based retrieval and a human-in-the-loop, LLM-assisted thematic analysis. AI-related discussion increased substantially over time, and 11,083 analytically coded posts revealed eight interconnected domains of experience. Everyday and social use was most prevalent (36.8 percent), while discourse increasingly shifted toward authenticity, personal control and safety, and future human roles. Across domains, adolescents questioned when AI should support or substitute for human thinking and creativity, how conversational AI changes relationships and perceptions of agency, what can still be considered authentic, who controls personal information and representation, and what opportunities and roles should remain human. These findings position adolescent AI use not simply as technology adoption, but as an emerging negotiation over AI's place and boundaries in everyday life. Supporting this transition will require developmentally appropriate AI literacy, psychological and social support, and AI systems and policies that protect adolescents' agency, privacy, relationships and opportunities for human development.

</details>


### [50] [Delegated Misalignment: How Multi-Agent Structures Amplify LLM Safety Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27900)
*Zonghao Ying,Jiaqi Yan,Huize Luo,Quanchen Zou,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示LLM安全对齐在单智能体设定下有效，但无法迁移到多智能体委托场景，提出"委托错位"现象及其两种失败机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于多智能体系统，主智能体拆解任务并委派给可使用外部工具的下属智能体，但现有安全对齐评测几乎只考虑单智能体威胁模型，将安全视为单个LLM的属性，作者认为这一假设不成立。

Method: 提出"委托错位"现象，识别两种失败机制——主智能体侧的"责任扩散"与下属智能体侧的"角色偏见顺从"，并设计三条件协议，在6个前沿LLM和49个危险任务上进行评测与消融实验。

Result: 委托显著放大端到端危害：DeepSeek-V3.2完整执行率从30.6%升至77.6%；同一模型不同角色表现差异巨大（GPT-5单智能体22.5% vs.下属61.2%）；单层防御各自失效甚至适得其反。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区超越单模型对齐，转向复合安全机制，以应对大规模部署的多智能体LLM系统风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems where a principal agent decomposes tasks and delegates them to subordinate agents that may invoke external tools. Safety alignment, however, is still evaluated almost exclusively under a single-agent threat model, treating safety as a property of the individual LLM. We show that this assumption breaks down: \emph{individual safety alignment fails to transfer to multi-agent settings}. Two failure mechanisms emerge under delegation: \emph{responsibility diffusion} on the principal side and \emph{role-bias compliance} on the subordinate side, jointly converting language-level refusal into actionable harm. We refer to this phenomenon as \textit{delegated misalignment} and study it through a three-condition protocol across 6 frontier LLMs on 49 hazardous tasks. Delegation amplifies end-to-end harm substantially: DeepSeek-V3.2's full-execution rate rises from 30.6\% to 77.6\% once delegation is introduced, and the same model behaves very differently across roles (GPT-5: 22.5\% as a single agent vs.\ 61.2\% as a subordinate). Ablations further show that standard single-layer defenses each fail on their own and can even backfire. We call on the community to move beyond per-model alignment and toward composite safety mechanisms before multi-agent LLM systems are deployed at scale.

</details>


### [51] [Learning When Not to Listen: Selective Anti-Interference Pretraining for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27925)
*Jinchang Zhu,Haowei He,Yi Ding,Rong Fu,Nie Xiaojian,Shuangyong Song,Zhongjiang He,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPAR目标，通过门控机制选择性抵抗远前缀干扰，提升长上下文鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型会过度依赖无关前缀，即使局部上下文已足够预测目标词，远距离无关token扰动仍会改变预测，在长/杂乱上下文中尤为严重。

Method: SPAR同时输入原序列和仅改动远前缀的破坏版本，用短上下文充分性门控和门控KL目标，稳定局部已支持的预测；门控基于模型估计远前缀是否提供额外信息。

Result: 机制分析显示门控能识别局部充分token并显著降低其前缀敏感性。在Qwen2.5-0.5B/3B、Llama-3.2-1B/3.1-8B、GPT2-XL的持续训练中提升RULER；预训练在RULER与NoLiMa上也有效。

Conclusion: 选择性抗干扰是一种有效的目标级信号，可提升模型上下文使用的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Language models can over-condition on irrelevant preceding text: predictions already supported by local context may still change when distant, unrelated prefix tokens are perturbed. This interference is especially consequential in long, packed, or distractor-heavy contexts, where useful evidence and irrelevant spans coexist. We propose Selective Prefix Anti-Interference Regularization (SPAR), a pretraining objective for selective anti-interference. SPAR runs the original sequence and a corrupt-prefix input in which only the far prefix is changed, then uses a short-context sufficiency gate and a gated KL objective to stabilize locally supported suffix predictions. The gate operationalizes a model-based estimate of whether the far prefix supplies additional information about the target token. Mechanism analyses show that the gate identifies locally sufficient tokens and sharply reduces prefix sensitivity on gate-selected suffix tokens. In continued training on pretrained base models, SPAR improves RULER across Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-3B, Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B, and GPT2-XL under equal counted training compute; pretraining experiments further show gains on both RULER and NoLiMa. These results show that selective anti-interference is an effective objective-level signal for robust context use.

</details>


### [52] [Risk-Controlled KV-Cache Eviction: From Memory Budgets to Risk Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27981)
*Beomgu Kang,SoJin Yun,Hojoon Kim,Hyunseok Seo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将KV-cache驱逐重新表述为部署风险控制问题，用有限样本保证的后验认证方法选择保留策略，在可靠契约下能给出不同模型/任务上可信的KV内存操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KV-cache驱逐只按平均质量-内存权衡评估，但小的平均损失可能掩盖部分请求的严重效用退化，缺乏面向部署的可靠性保证。

Method: 定义相对全KV推理超过容忍度的“实质性退化”事件，将部署风险定义为其出现频率；在给定目标风险与置信度要求下，使用压缩器无关的后验认证程序，从校准数据中选择带有限样本保证的保留策略，若无策略通过则回退全KV。

Result: 在多种驱逐方法、Llama与Mistral模型、LongBench与RULER-32K上，同一契约导致不同驱逐水平：Llama上LongBench可认证SnapKV 75%保留，但RULER-32K无可认证压缩策略并触发全KV回退；经验退化率低于5%目标的策略仍可能未通过有限样本认证；Llama LongBench上经验阈值会选择未认证策略，导致比固定预算方法少保留5-10个百分点缓存。

Conclusion: 该框架把部署级可靠性要求转化为KV内存操作点，强调了带统计保证的认证与仅凭经验平均权衡之间的显著差异。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction is typically evaluated through average quality-memory trade-offs, yet a small average loss can hide requests whose utility degrades materially. We reformulate eviction as a deployment risk-control problem: a material degradation occurs when eviction lowers task utility by more than a deployment-specified tolerance relative to full-KV inference on the same request, and deployment risk is the population frequency of such events. Given a reliability contract specifying a target risk level and confidence requirement, we use a compressor-agnostic post-hoc certification procedure to select a retention policy from calibration data with a finite-sample guarantee, falling back to full KV when no compressed policy is certified. Across multiple eviction methods, Llama and Mistral models, and LongBench and RULER-32K, the same contract supports substantially different levels of eviction: on Llama, it certifies SnapKV at 75% retention on LongBench but no tested compressed policy on RULER-32K, triggering full-KV fallback. Policies with empirical degradation rates below the 5% target can still fail finite-sample certification; on Llama LongBench, empirical thresholding selects uncertified policies that retain 5-10 percentage points less cache across fixed-budget methods. The proposed framework converts a deployment-level reliability requirement into a KV-memory operating point.

</details>


### [53] [Evaluating Feedback Focus and Pedagogical Adaptivity in LLM-Generated Feedback on Student Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28026)
*Norah Almousa,Shayan Peyghambari Oskoui,Raquel Coelho,Gayle Rogers,Xiang Lorraine Li,Diane Litman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究前沿LLM生成的反馈是否在反馈焦点与适应性上反映专家教师的教学实践，构建FeedType基准并系统评估六个LLM在三种提示策略下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往评估关注反馈特征、对学习的影响及反馈目标，但反馈焦点与适应性被大幅忽视，缺乏对LLM反馈与专家教师教学实践对齐程度的系统分析。

Method: 采用并改进Narciss的反馈分类法，形成七种反馈焦点类型，对三门大学写作课程的教师与LLM反馈进行标注；发布FeedType基准，涵盖六个LLM在三种提示策略下的反馈，分析焦点类型的覆盖与分布，并考察LLM是否跨草稿阶段与学生表现水平进行适应性调整。

Result: 多数LLM覆盖了大部分反馈焦点类型，但未能反映教师的反馈分布，且适应性水平参差不齐，没有任何模型能匹配教师的适应性行为。

Conclusion: LLM反馈在教学对齐上仍存在明显不足，FeedType基准可支持未来关于LLM反馈生成中教学对齐的研究。

Abstract: We investigate whether state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) generate feedback that reflects the pedagogical practices of expert teachers in terms of feedback focus and adaptivity. Previous evaluation efforts have examined feedback characteristics, its impact on learning, and its target, yet the focus of feedback and its adaptivity remains largely overlooked. To bridge this gap, we adopt and refine Narciss's taxonomy into seven feedback focus types to annotate teacher and LLM-generated feedback across three university writing courses. We release FeedType, a benchmark containing annotated teacher and LLM feedback from six LLMs under three prompting strategies. We assess the coverage and distribution of feedback focus types, and examine whether LLMs adapt their feedback across draft stages and student performance levels as an expert instructor does. Our findings show that while most LLMs cover most feedback focus types, they fail to reflect teacher feedback distributions and show varying levels of adaptivity, with none matching the teachers' adaptive behavior. We believe FeedType will support future research on pedagogical alignment in LLM feedback generation.

</details>


### [54] [How Much Were You Told? Measuring External Information in Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28041)
*Matthieu Dubois,Pablo Piantanida,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Conditioning，一种无监督信息论估计器，通过度量评审中外部信息含量来区分完全委托LLM生成的评审与机器润色的评审，而非仅看表面形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议政策区分\"用LLM润色自己的评审\"与\"委托LLM生成批评\"，但现有AI文本检测(ATD)方法主要测量表面形式，而非内容来源。

Method: 度量评审所携带的\"外部信息\"——即无法由被评审论文和通用评审指令解释的信息。提出Self-Conditioning：在生成上下文下计算评审似然，并与在该上下文加入从评审本身提取的提示后的似然进行比较。

Result: 在IntelLabs同行评审基准上，Self-Conditioning区分完全委托与机器润色评审的AUC最高达1.0，且对表面改写基本不敏感；随着生成器获得更多外部信息，其分数单调向人类区间移动，优于标准ATD基线。高温采样可规避该估计器，但代价是输出质量下降。

Conclusion: 相比表面形式的ATD，基于外部信息含量的Self-Conditioning能更本质地识别评审内容来源，为会议政策执行提供更有效的检测手段，但存在高温采样规避的局限。

Abstract: Conference policies distinguish using Large Language Models (LLMs) to polish one's own review from delegating the critique, but current Artificial Text Detection (ATD) methods largely measure surface form rather than the origin of its content. We instead measure the external information carried by a review: information not explained by the reviewed paper and a generic reviewing instruction. We propose Self-Conditioning, an unsupervised information-theoretic estimator that compares the likelihood of a review under its production context with its likelihood when that context is augmented with hints extracted from the review itself. On the IntelLabs peer-review benchmark, Self-Conditioning separates fully-delegated from machine-polished reviews with AUC up to $1.0$ while remaining largely insensitive to surface rewriting. Moreover, as generators receive increasing amounts of externally-provided information, their scores move monotonically towards the human regime, unlike standard ATD baselines. High-temperature sampling can evade the estimator, but at the cost of output quality.

</details>


### [55] [TEMPS: Temporal Sentence Embeddings for Temporal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28048)
*Mourad Hassani,Julien Romero,Amel Bouzeghoub,Christian Jacquelinet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEMPS通过为冻结的语义检索器添加模块化时间分支，解决稠密检索与RAG在时间维度上的失配问题，显著提升时间检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代IR系统很少表示时间，但在临床、新闻和法律检索中，事件发生时间决定文档相关性；现有稠密检索器与RAG管线在主题上匹配良好但时间上表现差，会返回主题相关却时间错误的文档，因此需要独立衡量文本时间对齐程度的方法。

Method: 提出Temporal Textual Similarity（TTS）任务，衡量两个锚定文本在时间上的对齐程度，独立于主题相似性；并提出TEMPS，一个模块化时间分支，附着于冻结的语义检索器。它将锚定时间表达解析为区间，将每个区间与高斯分布做矩匹配，由此产生排序信号以监督锚点日期条件编码器，推理时将时间分数与语义分数融合。时间分数采用分布嵌入的高斯-KL包含度量，TEMPS的新增在于grounding与矩匹配监督，因依赖grounding提供监督，训练无需人工标注时间数据。

Result: 在三个时间基准上，TEMPS对每个测试的语义backbone都提升了MRR；在TS-Retriever上，将R@1从先前时间SOTA的19.92提升至25.39。

Conclusion: TEMPS表明可通过将时间表达grounding与矩匹配监督结合到冻结语义检索器上，在无需人工时间标注的情况下有效弥补检索系统的时间盲区，并通过分数融合稳定提升时间检索性能。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval (IR) systems rarely represent time, yet many information needs depend on it: in clinical, journalistic, and legal search, when an event occurred can decide whether a document is relevant. Dense retrievers and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines match queries to documents well on topic but poorly on time, so they surface content that is on-topic yet temporally wrong. We introduce Temporal Textual Similarity (TTS), a task that measures how well two anchored texts align in time, independent of their topical similarity. We then present TEMPS (Temporal Embedding Model for Precise Search), a modular temporal branch that attaches to a frozen semantic retriever and trains on that signal. It resolves anchored temporal expressions to intervals and moment-matches each one to a Gaussian; the resulting ordering supervises an anchor-date-conditioned encoder, whose score we fuse with the semantic score at inference. Grounding supplies the supervision, so training uses no hand-labeled temporal data. The temporal score itself is the Gaussian-KL inclusion measure from distributional embeddings; what TEMPS adds is the grounding and the moment-matched supervision. On three temporal benchmarks, TEMPS improves MRR for every semantic backbone tested and, on TS- Retriever, lifts R@1 from 19.92 to 25.39 over the prior temporal state of the art.

</details>


### [56] [A Native-Reference Coordinate Geometry for L2 Pronunciation Deviation Using Self-Supervised Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28060)
*Tina Raissi,Nhan Phan,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"母语参照坐标几何"方法，将语音自监督模型中的音素信息转化为可解释的二语发音评估指标：用母语语音的音素类平均构造低维参照子空间，以二语语音到匹配母语音素坐标的距离评估发音，无需平行录音或发音标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音模型虽编码丰富音素信息，但如何将其转化为可解释的二语（L2）自发语音发音评估指标仍不清楚。

Method: 以母语语音中各类音素的平均向量定义低维母语参照子空间，将L2语音按到对应母语音素类坐标的距离进行评估。与既有基于距离的方法不同，该方法不依赖语言内容匹配的平行录音，也不需要专门的发音标签。

Result: 在多种自监督编码器和建模选择下，母语参照距离与口语熟练度呈最高约 -0.5 的负Spearman相关，即熟练度越高的说话者越接近母语参照空间。

Conclusion: 母语参照距离可作为无需平行录音与发音标注的可解释二语发音评估指标，且熟练度越高者越靠近母语参照空间。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech models encode rich phonetic information, but it remains unclear how to transform this information into interpretable metrics for second-language (L2) pronunciation assessment in spontaneous speech. We propose a native-reference coordinate geometry in which phone-class averages from native speech define a low-dimensional reference subspace, and L2 speech is evaluated by its distance to matching native phone-class coordinates. Unlike prior distance-based approaches, our method does not require parallel recordings with matched linguistic content or dedicated pronunciation labels. Across different self-supervised encoders and modeling choices, the resulting native-reference distances show negative Spearman correlations up to -0.5 with speaking proficiency, indicating that higher-proficiency speakers tend to lie closer to the native-reference space.

</details>


### [57] [Reference-Based Analysis of Coherence and Diversity in Open-Ended Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28080)
*Esteban Garcés Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于参考的框架，从三个视角考察开放文本生成中连贯性与多样性同人类感知质量的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生成文本的不同属性（连贯性、多样性）如何与人类感知质量相关联。

Method: 基于参考的框架，三视角：1) 将连贯性/多样性的演化与人类轨迹对齐；2) 将其摘要与同提示的人类续写对比；3) 估计其在人类参考分布下的似然。

Result: 多样性对齐与均值比较能捕捉质量相关变异；时间对齐相对简单基线无预测优势；参考似然与评分正相关，结果随参考配置和评分范围变化。

Conclusion: 提供结构化方法考察连贯性与多样性同人类判断的关系，并区分与人类参考的相似性与质量本身。代码见 https://github.com/EstebanGarces/likely_human。

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended text generation involves understanding how different properties of a continuation relate to its perceived quality. We present a reference-based framework for examining coherence and diversity through three perspectives: aligning their evolution with human trajectories, comparing their summaries with a human continuation of the same prompt, and estimating their likelihood under a human reference distribution. Experiments with human quality ratings suggest that diversity-based alignment and mean-based comparisons capture quality-related variation, although the comparisons do not establish a predictive advantage for temporal alignment over simpler baselines. Reference likelihood also shows positive associations with ratings, with results varying across reference configurations and scoring horizons. Together, these analyses provide a structured way to examine how measured coherence and diversity relate to human judgments, while distinguishing similarity to human references from quality itself. Code and analysis resources are available at https://github.com/EstebanGarces/likely_human.

</details>


### [58] [Exact Feedback Is Not Control: Evaluating Text-based Closed-Loop Revision in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28150)
*Haitong Jiang,Chunlin Liu,Yile Wang,Yuhong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出固定预算、确定性验证器的修订协议，提供精确反馈以隔离模型的修订行为，发现精确反馈虽使修订错误可观测，但无法使闭环可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环修订在LLM应用中日益普及，但失败可能源于反馈不完整，也可能源于模型对正确反馈的无效响应；需要分离反馈质量与模型行为来定位失败根源。

Method: 设计固定预算修订协议，使用确定性验证器报告所有剩余违规（精确长度、词汇与组合约束），固定反馈正确性与完整性以隔离模型侧修订行为；在19个开源/闭源模型上做控制实验，并采用匹配状态干预。

Result: 控制器级平均最终联合成功率在17.4%–99.8%之间，相同初始草稿下跨模型差距显著；模型对精确反馈有可复现的特异性响应。失败轨迹常重复早期输出，先前重复与更低恢复能力相关。移除较早对话（保持当前草稿与反馈不变）改变重复逃逸但未可靠提升最终成功率，效果依赖模型、任务与触发状态构成。

Conclusion: 精确反馈能让修订错误变得可观测，但无法使闭环可靠；后训练与规模改变模型响应却不必然趋向精确纠正。

Abstract: Closed-loop revision is increasingly used in large language model (LLM) applications, but failures may reflect incomplete feedback or ineffective responses to correct feedback. We introduce a fixed-budget revision protocol with deterministic verifiers that report all remaining violations across exact-length, lexical, and compositional constraints. Fixing feedback correctness and completeness isolates model-side revision behavior. Across 19 open- and closed-source models, controller-level mean final joint success ranges from 17.4% to 99.8%, with substantial cross-model gaps persisting under identical initial drafts. Controlled experiments reveal reproducible model-specific responses to exact feedback. Post-training and scale reshape these responses without consistently bringing them closer to exact correction. Across all constraint families, failed trajectories often repeat earlier outputs, and prior recurrence is associated with lower subsequent recoverability. Matched-state interventions show that removing earlier dialogue while holding the current draft and feedback fixed changes recurrence escape without reliably improving final success; effects depend on the model, task, and trigger-state composition. Exact feedback makes revision errors observable, but does not make the closed loop reliable. Code and reproduction instructions: https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/exact-feedback-code.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Poetry: Can Large Language Models Generate Classical Arabic Maqamat?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28245)
*AbdulRahman A. Morsy,Aya Zirikly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成玛卡梅（maqama）这一古典阿拉伯韵文文学体裁进行受控评测，比较五种模型在零样本、少样本与规则提示策略下的表现，结合人工标注与LLM评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在创意文本生成上表现强劲，但能否生成具文化根基、风格受限的古典文学形式仍未充分探索；既有研究多集中于现代语言变体和诗歌，而玛卡梅等古典散文传统几乎未被研究。

Method: 首次开展LLM玛卡梅生成的受控评测研究：比较五种模型在零样本、少样本和规则提示下的输出，从修辞丰富度、saj密度、结构连贯性和风格真实性等维度，采用人工标注与LLM-as-a-judge框架评估。

Result: 提示策略显著影响风格质量：少样本提示最稳定地提升saj密度，但对修辞和连贯性的影响因模型而异；最强模型（GPT-4o、GPT-5.4-mini）在规则提示下这两维度受益最大；零样本提示在五个模型上总体得分最高。模型间在与阿拉伯玛卡梅传统的风格契合上存在系统性差异，并通过第二个独立LLM评判、配对显著性检验和非LLM的saj代理指标加以印证。

Conclusion: LLM尚难超越表层流畅而达到深层文学能力，提示策略对古典风格生成效果因模型和维度而异，表明文化根基与风格受限的文学生成仍是挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in creative text generation, yet their ability to produce culturally grounded and stylistically constrained literary forms remains underexplored. Prior work has focused largely on modern language varieties and poetry, while classical prose traditions such as maqama remain largely unstudied. The maqama is a classical literary genre characterized by rhymed prose (saj), dense rhetorical ornamentation, and episodic narrative structure, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating whether LLMs can move beyond surface fluency toward deeper literary competence. In this paper, we present the first controlled evaluation study of maqama generation with LLMs, comparing five models under zero-shot, few-shot, and rule-based prompting, and evaluating outputs through both human annotation and an LLM-as-a-judge framework across dimensions such as rhetorical richness, saj density, structural coherence, and stylistic authenticity. Our results show that prompting strategy plays a strong role in stylistic quality: few-shot prompting most consistently improves saj density, while its effects on rhetoric and coherence vary by model, with the strongest models (GPT-4o and GPT-5.4-mini) benefiting most from rule-based prompting on these dimensions, though zero-shot prompting yields the highest aggregate scores across all five models. We further observe systematic differences between models in stylistic alignment with Arabic maqama conventions, and corroborate our findings with a second independent LLM judge, paired statistical significance testing, and non-LLM proxy measures of saj.

</details>


### [60] [Complementary Roles of Activation and Parametric Memory in Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28250)
*Miaohe Niu,Runsong Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Bo Jin,Yucheng Qiao,Chunliang Zhang,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型中激活记忆与参数记忆在少样本学习中的相互作用，发现二者功能不同但可协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活记忆（KV缓存）和参数记忆（更新参数）在测试时被用于编码历史信息，但二者的相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 通过控制实验系统探究记忆在少样本学习中的作用，并进行神经元级分析。

Result: 激活记忆在事实回忆上更优，而参数记忆在任务学习上并未稳定超过激活记忆；复合任务Conditional Arithmetic需要两种记忆协同；两种记忆访问同一历史信息时激活不同神经元，结合时则同时招募两组神经元。

Conclusion: 单一记忆机制不足以完成复合任务，二者协作至关重要。

Abstract: At test time, large language models (LLMs) can encode historical information in activation memory (i.e., KV caches) and parametric memory (i.e., updated parameters). While activation memory is generally considered effective for factual recall and parametric memory for learning new tasks, their interplay remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of memory in few-shot learning through controlled experiments. We find that activation memory is superior for recalling facts, whereas parametric memory does not consistently outperform activation memory in task learning. Moreover, our experiments show that the composite task, Conditional Arithmetic, requires the synergy of both memory types. Through neuron-level analysis, we find that the model activates distinct sets of neurons when accessing the same historical information through activation versus parametric memory. When both memory types are combined, the model recruits neurons from both sets, which is crucial for solving Conditional Arithmetic. These findings suggest that neither memory mechanism alone is sufficient for this composite task, highlighting the importance of their collaboration.

</details>


### [61] [Predicting Quantization Price for Selecting PTQ Configurations Before Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28270)
*Junbin Qiu,Jian Mu,Weitong Zhang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将权重空间PTQ重新表述为部署前的配置选择问题，用"定价的分层输出误差"作为统一评分，通过误差协方差与下游曲率的价格来比较格式、粒度、量化器与变换等候选配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法各自预测退化的一部分（重建误差、Hessian敏感度、变换影响、下游损失），但通常在固定量化几何后评分，或局限在分隔的配置族内，无法在部署前统一比较不同配置的代价与误差。

Method: 把每个可接受的分层配置视为带部署成本的误差生成器，诱导分层输出误差协方差Σ_l(α_l)；由全精度到量化的前向KL推导价格（一阶项在参考模型处消去），得到ρ_l(α_l)=½Tr(Ĥ_l Σ̂_l(α_l))。重建与对角评分成为丢弃价格因子的简化代理；不同格式、码本、粒度与等价变换通过其诱导协方差与投入成本成为可比候选。迹缩减得到校准期价格表与预算引导的选择器，固定几何比特分配成为特例。

Result: 提出了基于价格的统一评分框架，将多种PTQ评分统一为带价格因子的协方差度量，并构造出校准期价格表与预算约束下的价格引导选择器。

Conclusion: 该框架把权重量化PTQ从"固定几何下的比特分配"扩展为"部署前的配置选择"，使量化器族、变换、格式等异构选择在同一价格准则下可比。

Abstract: Weight-space post-training quantization (PTQ) must choose finite formats, granularities, quantizer families, transformations, and bits before the completed quantized model reveals its output-distribution drift. Existing PTQ methods predict important pieces of this degradation, including reconstruction error, Hessian sensitivity, transformation effects, and downstream loss, but these pieces are usually scored after fixing the quantization geometry or inside separate configuration families. We formulate weight-space PTQ as pre-deployment configuration selection using priced layer-output error. Each admissible layer configuration is treated as an error generator with a deployment cost, which induces a layer-output error covariance $\boldsymbolΣ_l(α_l)$, and the full-precision model prices that covariance by downstream curvature, $\widehatρ_l(α_l)=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_l\,\widehat{\boldsymbolΣ}_l(α_l)\right)$. The price follows from full-precision-to-quantized forward KL, whose first-order term cancels at the reference model. It turns reconstruction and diagonal scores into reduced proxies that drop price factors, while finite formats, codebooks, granularities, and equivalent transformations become comparable candidates through the covariances they induce and the costs they pay. A trace reduction then yields a calibration-time price table and a budgeted price-guided selector, making fixed-geometry bit allocation a special case rather than the organizing problem.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Efficient Reasoning: Learning Causal Shortcuts for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28272)
*Dian Jin,Kairong Han,Baohong Li,Xinpeng Dong,Zijing Hu,Nuanqiao Shan,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出因果捷径学习(CSL)框架，通过对推理引导token进行并行优先掩码训练，提升扩散语言模型的推理准确率与收敛效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(DLM)采用双向注意力，探索空间远大于自回归模型(ARM)，在随机掩码下难以聚焦于推理引导token。作者定义“因果捷径”为覆盖全序列、能显式引导正确推理路径的token链，并发现其对DLM的推理精度与收敛速度有显著提升作用。

Method: 提出CSL框架：先用逐步token提取流程从数据中抽取因果捷径，再在训练时对这些token施加并行优先掩码(parallel prioritized masking)，从而高效、准确地沿因果捷径收敛到正确答案。

Result: 在多个推理基准和两个基座模型上，CSL一致优于现有SFT变体基线，较仅SFT模型平均提升1.92%，在MATH-500上提升高达4.20%。

Conclusion: 因果捷径能有效引导DLM推理，CSL框架通过优先掩码训练显著提升准确率与收敛效率，验证了该方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have attracted significant attention for their strong reasoning ability. However, under a bidirectional attention mechanism, DLMs operate over an exponentially large exploration space compared to autoregressive models (ARMs), making it challenging to focus on reasoning-guiding tokens under random masking. We define causal shortcuts as token chains that cover the full sequence and provide explicit guidance towards correct reasoning trajectories. We analyze the effects of causal shortcuts on the reasoning accuracy and convergence speed of DLMs, and find that they largely improve answer convergence efficiency and generation accuracy. Motivated by this, we propose a Causal Shortcut Learning (CSL) Framework for DLMs. Specifically, we introduce a step-by-step token extraction procedure to extract causal shortcuts from data, and apply parallel prioritized masking on these tokens during training to enable efficient and accurate convergence to correct answers via causal shortcuts. Extensive experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks and two base models demonstrate that CSL consistently outperforms existing SFT-variant baselines, achieving an average improvement of $1.92\%$ over SFT-only models, and up to $4.20\%$ on MATH-500. The code is available at the \href{https://github.com/ZJUDianJin/Causal-Shortcuts-Learning}{https://github.com/ZJUDianJin/Causal-Shortcuts-Learning

</details>


### [63] [Computation Over Geometry: Meaning Identity Is Computed, Not Shipped in the Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28290)
*Jiaqi Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语义同一性并非独立编码句向量的几何属性，而是在两句话共享一次前向传播时“计算”出来的运算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索与RAG通常把“改述后语义是否相同”当作独立句向量的几何事实来处理，本文质疑这一前提是否成立。

Method: 在重叠匹配的PAWS-X上，对比独立编码向量（BGE、E5、GTE、MiniLM、E5-Mistral-7B及Llama 3、Mistral、Qwen末位状态）与联合前向传播探针；并测试线性/非线性配对读取器、重排序器、跨模型家族（因果LM、双向编码器、编码-解码器）及蒸馏实验。

Result: 独立编码器英文确认AUC仅0.55–0.65（稠密峰值0.70），独立末位状态与晚期融合接近随机；联合前向探针达0.90–0.96，伙伴打乱后崩溃，随规模饱和于约0.94。线性读取器无法解锁同一性，非线性配对读取器仅在完整PAWS训练集上部分恢复（0.68–0.87）。重排序器表现分化：BGE-reranker-large达0.94，MS-MARCO与Jina仅0.55–0.64。

Conclusion: 余弦相似度比较的是措辞邻域，语义同一性是一个廉价的“计算”运算符，而非任一句向量的固有属性；该关系可被独立训练的模型族共享并被联合读取器从无标注教师分数中蒸馏。

Abstract: Meaning identity (whether two sentences say the same thing after wording changes) is treated in retrieval and RAG as a geometric fact about independently encoded sentence vectors. We show that, for frozen off-the-shelf encoders and language models, it is not: identity is computed when both sentences share one forward pass, and is not a property of the embedding geometry those systems ship. On overlap-matched PAWS-X, purpose-built encoders (BGE, E5, GTE, MiniLM, E5-Mistral-7B) reach English confirm AUC only 0.55-0.65 (dense peak 0.70). Independently encoded last-token states of Llama 3, Mistral, and Qwen do no better; late fusion of the two vectors stays near chance. The same probe on a joint forward pass reaches 0.90-0.96 from 1.5B to 32B, collapses under partner shuffle, is mid-depth, saturates near 0.94 by 3B, and appears more weakly in GPT-2 XL (0.76). The gap holds beyond Llama-style models on other causal LMs, bidirectional encoders (DeBERTa, RoBERTa), and encoder-decoders (Flan-T5, T5, BART). Fixed or linear readers over frozen independent encodings never unlock identity; nonlinear pair readers recover part of it only on the full 49k-pair PAWS train split (0.68-0.87). Off-the-shelf rerankers split: BGE-reranker-large reaches 0.94, while MS-MARCO and Jina stay at 0.55-0.64. Independently trained families compute the same relation and a 1.5B joint reader can distill it from unlabelled teacher scores, while no linear function of the teachers own independent vectors can. Bi-encoders can be fine-tuned to fit PAWS (0.87-0.93), but transfer and STS-B suffer. Cosine compares wording neighbourhoods; identity is a cheap computed operator, not a property of either sentence vector.

</details>


### [64] [Digital diglossia: Arabic between X and Facebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28352)
*Fahad Al Hussen,Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对比X与Facebook上标准阿拉伯语（SA）和口语阿拉伯语（CA）的分布，发现话语类别与平台均显著影响双语体选择，数字时代重塑而非消解了双语体边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨数字平台（X vs. Facebook）如何影响阿拉伯语双语体（标准语与口语）的使用，填补数字话语领域中双语体分布研究空白。

Method: 用Python采集16754条公开帖，保留10000条为净数据集，分为7类话语（政治、科技、科学、商业、文化、娱乐、体育）。采用卡方检验与Cramer's V分析平台、话语类别与双语体选择的关联，并用二元逻辑回归及Platform×Discourse交互检验跨平台差异。

Result: 话语类别与双语体选择在X（χ²=600.35, p<.001, CV=.347）和Facebook（χ²=1249.52, p<.001, CV=.500）均显著相关；平台也与双语体选择相关（χ²=262.16, p<.001, CV=.162）。政治类中X使用SA的几率高于Facebook（OR=1.31），文化（OR=2.65）、娱乐（OR=6.34）、体育（OR=26.71）、科学（OR=0.41）、科技（OR=0.71）存在显著交互。

Conclusion: SA与CA的双语体使用丰富了数字话语研究，表明数字时代重塑而非侵蚀双语体边界，形成一种“重构的数字双语体”。

Abstract: This study highlights the distribution of Standard Arabic (SA; H(igh) variety) and Colloquial Arabic (CA; L(ow) variety) across X and Facebook. 16754 public posts were collected via Python, with 10000 retained as the net dataset. Posts were classified into 7 discourse categories: *politics, technology, science, business, culture, fun,* and *sports*. Bivariate analyses, including Chi-square tests and Cramer's V (CV), examined associations among platform, discourse category, and diglossic choice, while binary logistic regression with Platform x Discourse Category interactions tested whether these associations varied across platforms. Findings reveal that there are significant associations between discourse category and diglossic choice on X, chi-square(6, *N* = 5000) = 600.35, p < .001, CV = .347, and Facebook, chi-square(6, N = 5000) = 1249.52, p < .001, CV = .500. Across platforms, platform was also associated with diglossic choice, chi-square(1, N = 10000) = 262.16, p < .001, CV = .162. Binary logistic regression further shows higher odds of SA use on X than Facebook in the political reference category (*OR* = 1.31, p = .0028), with significant platform-by-domain interactions for Culture (OR = 2.65), Fun (*OR* = 6.34), Sports (*OR* = 26.71), Science (OR = 0.41), and Technology (OR = 0.71). The study concludes that the diglossic use of SA and CA contributes to the growing body of research on digital discourse, unveiling that the digital age reshapes but does not erode diglossic boundaries, giving rise instead to a reconfigured digital diglossia.

</details>


### [65] [Fine-Tuning LLMs for Translation: General Forgetting Mitigation Does Not Preserve MT-Specific Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28395)
*Niklas Scholz,David Thulke,Abdallah Nasir,Will Allred,Evgeny Matusov,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究微调LLM做翻译时的灾难性遗忘，评估多种缓解方法对通用能力与MT特定指令跟随（MT-IF）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻译数据微调提升质量但导致灾难性遗忘，已有方法仅用通用基准评估，未验证是否适用于翻译场景及MT-IF任务。

Method: 对比基于辅助数据、模型输出和基座参数的缓解方法；先在Llama 3.2 1B Instruct筛选，再在Llama 3.1 8B Instruct上用双向阿英/西英数据微调。

Result: EWC在通用能力保持上最优（8B西语模型通用基准平均仅降1.7分，标准微调降11.0）；但其形式与语法性别控制分数与标准微调接近。仅数据混合含控制任务样本能保留控制能力，但增益无法迁移到同任务新提示。

Conclusion: 通用基准结论可部分迁移至MT微调，但MT-IF控制能力的保持与通用能力保持并不一致，需专门评估。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on parallel data improves translation quality but can cause catastrophic forgetting. Mitigation methods are generally evaluated by retention on general benchmarks. We ask whether these findings transfer to machine translation (MT) fine-tuning and to MT-specific instruction following (MT-IF): instructions that modify a translation, such as formality, grammatical gender, and length control. We compare methods anchored to auxiliary data, to model outputs, and to the base model parameters, first in a screening study with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct, then on Llama 3.1 8B Instruct fine-tuned on bidirectional Arabic-English or Spanish-English data. Elastic Weight Consolidation preserves general capabilities best in both stages; on the 8B Spanish model the average score on general benchmarks drops 1.7 points versus 11.0 for standard fine-tuning, yet its scores for formality and grammatical gender control remain close to standard fine-tuning. Only data mixing with control-task examples preserves these controls, but its gains do not transfer to unseen prompts for the same task.

</details>


### [66] [Agent-Editing World Model: Rethinking World Modeling for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28416)
*Shuang Sun,Guoxin Chen,Fanzhe Meng,Jia Deng,Huatong Song,Jinhao Jiang,Wayne Xin Zhao,Hongteng Xu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)，通过编辑推理-动作链而非模拟工具响应来改进LLM智能体在长程任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言世界模型主要预测环境观测，但重建高熵、依赖执行的工具响应价值有限；同时智能体存在"任务状态污染"问题，即无依据的假设和过时计划留存于历史中并扭曲后续决策。

Method: AEWM建模推理和动作如何塑造未来任务进展。包含Action Judge（区分Critical、Exploratory、Noisy决策）与State Revision（从同一观测历史编辑噪声推理-动作续写）；EditAct将两者与真实执行结合，直接改变后续决策的状态基底。通过mid-training和SFT在搜索、终端、软件工程领域训练。

Result: Action Judge基准达70.5% macro-F1，超最强基线10.6分；三个智能体骨干、六个基准上EditAct平均提升3.2-6.7分；AEWM-RFT在三个领域比Self-RFT提升2.2-2.6分，且无需在线AEWM指导。

Conclusion: 编辑式世界模型（改变状态而非模拟响应）能有效缓解任务状态污染，显著提升智能体长程任务性能，并可蒸馏至离线训练。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to tackle long-horizon tasks across diverse environments. To further improve agent performance, existing language world models typically predict environment observations, yet reconstructing high-entropy, execution-dependent tool responses offers limited value when real feedback is available. Meanwhile, agents suffer from \emph{task-state contamination}, where unsupported assumptions and outdated plans persist in history and distort subsequent decisions. We propose the \textbf{Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)}, which models how reasoning and actions shape future task progress rather than simulating tool responses. AEWM combines \textbf{Action Judge} to distinguish \textsc{Critical}, \textsc{Exploratory}, and \textsc{Noisy} decisions with \textbf{State Revision} to edit noisy reasoning--action continuations from the same observed history. \textbf{EditAct} integrates these capabilities with real execution, directly changing the state underlying subsequent decisions rather than merely providing critiques. We train AEWM across Search, Terminal, and Software Engineering through mid-training and supervised fine-tuning. AEWM achieves 70.5\% macro-F1 on our Action Judge benchmark, exceeding the strongest frontier baseline by 10.6 points. Across six benchmarks and three agent backbones, EditAct improves average scores by 3.2--6.7 points over the strongest baseline. Furthermore, rejection sampling fine-tuning on verified EditAct trajectories, termed \textbf{AEWM-RFT}, improves over Self-RFT by 2.2--2.6 points across three domains without online AEWM guidance.

</details>


### [67] [Cross-Scale Transfer Learning for Depression Severity Prediction: From PHQ-8 to HAMD-17 Across Languages and Clinical Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28430)
*Wenjie Feng,Sahba Zojaji,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出序列LoRA协议，用Qwen3从英语DAIC-WOZ迁移到中文PDCH，实现数据稀缺下的抑郁严重度评分预测，两模型规模均取得最佳MAE/RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床访谈文本下的连续抑郁评分预测受数据稀缺制约；跨语言、跨量表、跨范式的迁移尚缺乏针对性研究。

Method: Qwen3骨干+有界回归头，先在DAIC-WOZ（PHQ-8）微调，LoRA适配器初始化PDCH（HAMD-17）微调，重初始化量表专用头；患者级分层5折2次重复交叉验证；含消融（监督对齐、翻译输入、顺序反转等）。

Result: 最优MAE/RMSE/macro-F1：Qwen3-0.6B为4.96/6.59/0.36，1.7B为4.38/5.62/0.46，优于仅目标域训练与非LLM基线；对齐源监督估计最佳，中文原生输入优于机器翻译，反转顺序无显著增益。

Conclusion: 为探索性单中心内部评估，未证明筛查或诊断效用，也未分离量表、语言与范式迁移的贡献；首次评估该DAIC-WOZ→PDCH序列迁移设置。

Abstract: This work addresses continuous depression-severity score prediction from clinical interview transcripts under data scarcity. We propose a sequential low-rank adaptation (LoRA) protocol for cross-scale transfer: a Qwen3 backbone with a bounded regression head is first fine-tuned on the English DAIC-WOZ dataset (189 avatar-mediated sessions, PHQ-8), and the adapter then initializes fine-tuning on the Chinese PDCH dataset (100 real clinical consultations, HAMD-17), where a reinitialised, scale-specific head predicts the clinician-assigned score. All configurations use patient-level stratified 5-fold, 2-repeat cross-validation. On the data-scarce HAMD-17 target, the sequential protocol attains the best point-estimate MAE , RMSE, and macro-$F_1$ on both 0.6B and 1.7B backbones, outperforming target-only training and non-LLM baselines---4.96/6.59/0.36 with Qwen3-0.6B and 4.38/5.62/0.46 with Qwen3-1.7B. Ablations suggest that correctly aligned source supervision gives the best point estimates (unsupervised exposure and shuffled-label controls also show partial gains), that native-Chinese target input outperforms machine-translated English input, and that the reversed order yields no clear gain within run-to-run variance. The study is an exploratory, single-site internal evaluation: it does not establish screening or diagnostic utility, nor separately identify the contribution of the scale, language, or paradigm shifts. To our knowledge, no prior study evaluates this specific DAIC-WOZ-to-PDCH sequential transfer setting.

</details>


### [68] [Contrastive Learning for Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28471)
*Peter Kirby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，在作者验证任务中，对比学习优于基于分类的方法，并据此开发出在PAN21任务上达到98.4%准确率的ModernBERT双编码器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究作者验证（authorship verification）任务中不同学习范式的效果差异，并寻找影响模型性能的关键因素。

Method: 比较对比学习与基于分类的方法，并分析损失函数、批大小、训练时长、预训练模型、输入上下文长度及随机文本片段数据增强等关键因素，据此构建ModernBERT双编码器模型。

Result: 对比学习在测试设置下优于基于分类的方法；所开发的ModernBERT双编码器模型在PAN21作者验证任务上达到98.4%准确率。

Conclusion: 对比学习是作者验证的有效范式，合理配置损失函数、批大小等训练因素可显著提升性能，最终模型在PAN21任务上取得优异结果。

Abstract: Our results show that contrastive learning outperforms a classification-based approach to authorship verification under the tested settings. We identify loss function, batch size, training duration, pre-trained model, input context length, and random text span data augmentation as important factors of model performance. Based on these considerations, we develop a ModernBERT Bi-Encoder model that achieves 98.4% accuracy on the PAN21 authorship verification task.

</details>


### [69] [Six Layers Less: Encoder Pruning for Whisper with Label-Free Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27980)
*Rasmus Aagaard,Nicki Skafte Detlefsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过留一法 WER 评估对 Whisper encoder 层进行剪枝（移除6层，占编码器18.5%），无需自定义推理代码，并通过单语无标注语音蒸馏恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper 等 ASR 模型解码器剪枝已被广泛采用（如 turbo 从32层降到4层），但编码器压缩进展有限，原因之一是压缩后通常需要自定义推理实现才能获益。

Method: 对编码器层做 leave-one-layer-out，按移除单层后 WER 变化排序，去掉影响最小的6层；随后用无标注单语语音进行蒸馏，以弥补零样本剪枝带来的性能下降。

Result: 剪枝后仅需更浅编码器，无需自定义推理。四语言平均 WER 零样本为21.9%，蒸馏后降至20.1%，基线为18.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种简单可直接部署的 Whisper 编码器层剪枝与蒸馏恢复方法，并开源代码与剪枝模型。

Abstract: Pruning large pre-trained transformer-based ASR models such as OpenAI's Whisper has seen great adoption, as pruning the decoder led to significant end-to-end transcription speedups. For instance, the {\tt whisper-large-v3-turbo} variant reduced the decoder from 32 to 4 layers, while Distill-Whisper similarly reduced the decoder to only 2 layers. Although some attention has been put towards reducing the size of the encoder, no approach has seen wide adoption. This could be due to the need for custom inference implementations to take advantage of the compressed model. We present an approach that ranks encoder layers by the leave-one-layer-out change in Word Error Rate (WER). The six layers that cause the least change are removed, corresponding to $18.5\%$ of the encoder stack. The pruned model requires no custom inference code as it is simply a more shallow encoder with fewer layers. We further distill using unlabeled monolingual speech data to recover performance degradation caused by the zero-shot layer pruning. Mean WER across four languages increases to $20.1\%$ after distillation, compared to $21.9\%$ zero-shot, going from a baseline of $18.2\%$. We release all of our code (https://github.com/rasgaard/whisper-encoder-layer-prune) and the pruned model (https://huggingface.co/rasgaard/whisper-large-v3-turbo-encoder-pruned).

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [When Post-Processing Fairness Constraints Help and When They Harm: Evidence from Eight Cross-Domain Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26955)
*Nithin Raghava Ramachandra Narla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAPE四阶段框架，评估ThresholdOptimizer在8个领域上的公平性干预效果，发现效果取决于基线差异幅度，单次部署审计不可靠，主张基线差异筛查与持续监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产ML公平性审计通常仅在部署时、单一领域进行一次，但重训练或用户群变化会使公平性偏移，且单领域验证的干预难以泛化到组织部署的异质领域。

Method: FAPE四阶段框架，评估单一后处理干预Fairlearn的ThresholdOptimizer，跨8个领域评估（刑事司法、收入预测、法律录取、信贷、农业贷款、多领域基准语料、医疗、教育），以人口统计均等与均等化几率差评分，辅以差别影响比和精度损失；引入最小群组规模和留出数据阈值两项测量检查，并用CUSUM监控器在模拟偏移下测试。

Result: 干预效果与基线差异幅度相关：14个高差异案例中9个改善、4个接近公平案例中3个恶化；5个高差异例外均被两项测量检查之一逆转；CUSUM监控器能区分从未达标与先达标后回退的模型。

Conclusion: 单次部署时审计不可靠，应进行基线差异筛查并持续监控公平性。

Abstract: Fairness audits in production ML typically occur once, at deployment, on a single domain. Both fail in practice: fairness can shift after retraining or a changing user base, and interventions validated on one dataset are rarely tested across the heterogeneous domains an organization deploys. We present FAPE (Fairness Auditing for Production Environments), a four-stage framework evaluating a single post-processing intervention, Fairlearn's ThresholdOptimizer, across eight domain evaluations: criminal justice, income prediction, legal admissions, credit lending, agricultural lending, a multi-domain benchmark corpus, healthcare, and education. Each is scored on demographic parity and equalized odds difference, plus disparate impact ratio and accuracy cost where computable. Intervention effectiveness tracks baseline disparity magnitude: across model-domain pairs the constraint improved disparity in 9 of 14 high-disparity cases and worsened it in 3 of 4 near-fair ones. Each of the five high-disparity exceptions reverses under one of two measurement checks, a minimum group size or thresholds fit on held-out data. A CUSUM monitor started at deployment, tested on a simulated shift, separates constrained models that never met a 0.1 parity convention from those that met it and later regressed. A single deployment-time audit is therefore an unreliable guide, which argues for baseline-disparity screening and continuous monitoring

</details>


### [71] [Memory Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28399)
*Jiale Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Memory Attention (MA)，用 token 索引的层特定记忆结合上下文键来构造注意力 value，替代专用 value 投影，并支持归一化折叠与 CPU offloading。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力 value 通常由上下文隐状态构造，但其中部分内容在不同上下文中可复用，存在冗余。

Method: 用层特定 token memory 提供 token 级表示，配合 contextual keys 保留上下文依赖，共同形成 value；推理时折叠归一化到记忆表，value 构造简化为查表加加法，并支持 CPU offloading 与预取。

Result: 在匹配训练 token 预算并增加记忆参数的情况下，多种注意力配置下语言建模与下游平均性能均有提升，同时减少 GPU 参数存储。

Conclusion: token 索引记忆可替代专用 value 投影，兼顾性能提升与推理效率。

Abstract: Language models typically construct attention values from contextual hidden states, even when some of their content may be reusable across contexts. We investigate whether token-indexed memory can replace the dedicated value projection when complemented by contextual information. We propose Memory Attention (MA), which forms values by combining layer-specific token memory with contextual keys. The memory supplies token-specific representations, while the keys preserve context dependence. At inference, normalization can be folded into the memory tables, reducing value construction to lookup and addition. Token-indexed retrieval also enables CPU offloading with prefetching, reducing GPU parameter storage. Under matched training token budgets and with additional memory parameters, experiments across attention configurations show improved language modeling and average downstream performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Spec2COBOLRot: An Agentic-AI Degradation Loop for Realistic COBOL Corpus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26835)
*Jean-Baptiste Espinasse,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种 agentic AI 流水线，通过规范驱动生成与迭代“降级”来生成贴近真实生产代码的 COBOL 程序，以结构复杂度指标衡量真实性。结果显示能稳定生成语法正确且结构复杂度接近真实的程序，但业务行为的保持无法保证，且独立优化结构指标存在偏离真实维护历史的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL 仍在广泛部署，但缺乏反映真实生产代码的代表性语料，导致现代化方法难以进行严格基准测试。

Method: 构建系统性 agentic AI 流水线：先进行规范驱动的程序生成，再结合从真实生产代码中提取的模式与复杂度目标，进行迭代式降级（degradation）来逼近生产代码结构。

Result: 在来自三个不同业务领域的程序上评估：流水线能可靠生成语法正确的程序，并使其结构复杂度转向更真实的水平；但业务行为的保持并不总能通过构造实现，且脱离业务逻辑单独追求结构指标，可能生成复杂度无法反映合理维护历史的程序。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了上述局限，并提出更现实的替代方向：沿着模拟的开发历史，从零开始生成遗留程序。

Abstract: COBOL remains widely deployed, yet representative corpora reflecting real production code are rarely available, limiting rigorous benchmarking of modernization approaches. We propose a systematic agentic AI pipeline for generating realistic COBOL programs, combining specification-driven generation with iterative degradation guided by patterns and complexity targets extracted from real production code. Here, realism is understood as structural fidelity to production code as captured by our metrics. We evaluate whether degradation reaches target complexity levels while preserving business behavior, and examine the limits of the approach, across three programs from distinct business domains. Results show the pipeline reliably produces syntactically valid programs and moves them toward realistic structural complexity. However, preserving business behavior is not always achieved by construction, and targeting structural metrics independently of business logic risks producing programs whose complexity does not reflect a plausible maintenance history. We discuss these limitations and outline a more realistic alternative as a direction for future work, generating legacy programs from scratch along a simulated development history.

</details>


### [73] [A Hybrid Rule-Based and AI-Augmented Framework for Automatic Failure Recovery in DevOps Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26838)
*Raju Chowdhury,Aniruddha Singh Gautam,Apurv Ghai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种混合模型，将确定性规则恢复与机器学习故障预测结合，实现DevOps环境的实时故障监控、识别、分类与自动恢复，MTTR降低94.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps部署中的自动故障恢复因系统复杂、工作量大以及传统规则或人工方法的局限而成为难点。

Method: 使用10万条分布式日志数据，构建确定性规则恢复与机器学习预测相结合的混合框架，评估Decision Tree、Random Forest、Logistic Regression、LightGBM、Autoencoder with BiLSTM等模型，进行实时故障识别、分类与恢复。

Result: Decision Tree在故障识别中表现最佳（F1 80.8%、召回80.0%、精确率81.6%、准确率89.4%）；自动恢复成功率达83.3%；MTTR下降94.6%，停机时间减少95.4%，年节省7,462。

Conclusion: 可解释的规则逻辑与自适应AI预测相结合，使框架更具韧性、运营效率和可扩展性，为DevOps环境提供有效的自主恢复方案（受控实验条件下评估）。

Abstract: Automatic failure recovery is another difficult area of DevOps deployments due to the complexity of the system, high workload and the limitations of the traditional rules-based or manual approach. This study proposes a hybrid model integrating deterministic, rule-based recovery with the assistance of machine learning by fault prediction to automatically monitor failures, identify, classify and recover failures in real time. To test the architecture, a distributed log dataset of 100,000 records was used for models such as Decision Tree, Random Forest, Logistic Regression, LightGBM, Autoencoder with BiLSTM. The Decision Tree had a strong performance in defect identification with an F1 score of 80.8%, recall of 80.0%, precision of 81.6%, and accuracy of 89.4%. An impressive 83.3% success rate was achieved by the automated recovery measures, resulting in a 94.6% decrease in mean time to recovery (MTTR), a 95.4% reduction in downtime, and annual savings of 7,462. The combination of explainable rule-based logic and adaptive AI prediction in the framework means it is more resilient, operationally efficient, and scalable, and an effective, autonomous approach to DevOps environments evaluated under controlled experimental conditions.

</details>


### [74] [Who Finishes the Job? A Study of Follow-Up Fixes and Commit Authorship on AI Coding Agent Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26847)
*Wannita Takerngsaksiri,Nhat Duong,Scott Barnett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 合并后的AI编码智能体PR比人类PR更常需要后续修复（1.62倍几率），但近70%的修复仍由同一智能体完成，76.4%的修复PR全程由智能体编写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体PR被合并通常被视为完成的工作，但已有研究指出合并后代码仍存在代码异味和静态分析问题。目前尚不清楚合并后的智能体PR多常被修复、以及实际由谁修复。

Method: 基于AIDev-pop数据集，追踪5个智能体（Codex、Copilot、Devin、Cursor、Claude Code）的6,774个已合并PR的后续修复，并以5,044个人类PR为基线。将每次合并链接到候选修复，用人工标注者和达到人类水平一致性的LLM评审验证（Cohen's Kappa=0.78 vs 0.77，Direct-fix精度90%），在PR和commit层面归因。

Result: (1) 合并智能体PR被验证修复的几率是同期同仓库人类PR的1.62倍；(2) 智能体合并中69.6%的验证修复来自同一智能体；(3) 76.4%的验证修复PR的所有commit均由智能体编写。

Conclusion: 智能体目前基本能"自己收尾"自己的工作，但其合并仍比人类合并更需要后续修复。

Abstract: AI coding agents now author a large share of pull requests (PRs) merged into popular open-source projects. A merged agent PR is usually considered finished work; yet, prior studies have reported issues in agent code after the merge (e.g., code smells and static-analysis issues). However, little is known about how often a merged agent PR is fixed afterward, and who actually authors the fixing. In this paper, we follow 6,774 merged agent PRs across five AI coding agents (OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, Devin, Cursor, and Claude Code) from the AIDev-pop dataset (open-source repositories with at least 500 stars) into their follow-up fixes, against a baseline of 5,044 contemporaneous human PRs from the same repositories. We link each merge to its candidate fixes, verify every candidate with human annotators and an LLM judge that matches human-level agreement (binary Cohen's Kappa=0.78$ against a human-human K=0.77$, Direct-fix precision 90%), and attribute the fixing work at the PR and the commit level. Our findings show that (1) merged agent PRs attract verified fixes at 1.62 times the odds of merged human PRs in the same repositories over the same period of time; (2) 69.6% of verified fixes in agent merges come from the same agent; and (3) 76.4% of the verified fix PRs are agent-authored throughout all commits. These results show that agents currently largely finish their own job, but their merges still require fixing more often than human merges.

</details>


### [75] [Kubernetes Misconfigurations in the Wild: Taxonomy, Evolution, and Automated Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27030)
*Mostafa Anouar Ghorab,Ahmad Abdel Latif,Mohamed Aymen Saied*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于2,662条Stack Overflow问题实证研究Kubernetes安全错误配置模式，并提出结合LLM上下文推理与模式校验的Kubecurity框架，将修复准确率提升至98.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes声明式配置模型易引入安全错误配置，威胁系统可靠性；现有检测工具虽存在，但错误配置模式与可扩展修复方法仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 对2,662条开发者上报的Stack Overflow问题开展实证研究，构建跨配置对象与类别的错误配置分类体系，分析严重性差异及孵化器与稳定阶段演化；在逐步丰富上下文条件下评估LLM自动修复，并提出基于官方Kubernetes规范的模式引导校验框架Kubecurity。

Result: 上下文强化可提升修复准确率，最佳独立模型达89.06%；结合上下文LLM推理与确定性模式校验后，修复准确率达98.50%，并显著减少新引入的错误配置。部分运维问题随项目成熟减少，但关键安全错误配置常持续或重现。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对Kubernetes安全错误配置的理解，并证明了混合式自动化修复方案在可靠性上的优势。

Abstract: Kubernetes is widely used to orchestrate cloud-native applications, yet its declarative configuration model often introduces security misconfigurations that threaten system reliability. Despite available detection tools, misconfiguration patterns and scalable remediation remain insufficiently understood.
  This paper presents an empirical study of Kubernetes security misconfigurations based on 2,662 developer-reported Stack Overflow issues. We derive a taxonomy of recurring security weaknesses across configuration objects and categories. We analyze severity variations and investigate how misconfigurations evolve between incubator and stable project stages. Findings show that while some operational issues decrease as projects mature, critical security misconfigurations often persist or reappear.
  We then evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated remediation under progressively enriched contextual conditions. Contextual grounding improves correction accuracy, with the best standalone model achieving 89.06%. To enhance structural correctness and schema compliance, we introduce Kubecurity, a schema-guided validation framework based on official Kubernetes specifications.
